(<code>aws-external-anthropic.{region}.api.aws</code>) resolves to AWS IP ranges. See the <a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/aws-ip-ranges.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">AWS IP address ranges</a> for inbound allowlisting.</p></div></aside>
<h2 id="inbound-ip-addresses" class="group">Inbound IP addresses</h2>
<p>These are the IP addresses where Anthropic services receive incoming connections.</p>
<h3 id="i-pv4" class="group">IPv4</h3>
<p><code>160.79.104.0/23</code></p>
<p><code>153.61.224.0/22</code></p>
<h3 id="i-pv6" class="group">IPv6</h3>
<p><code>2607:6bc0::/48</code></p>
<h2 id="outbound-ip-addresses" class="group">Outbound IP addresses</h2>
<p>These are the stable IP addresses that Anthropic uses for outbound requests (for example, when making MCP tool calls to external servers).</p>
<h3 id="i-pv4-2" class="group">IPv4</h3>
<p><code>160.79.104.0/21</code></p>
<p><code>153.61.208.0/20</code></p>
<h3 id="phased-out-ip-addresses" class="group">Phased out IP addresses</h3>
<p>The following IP addresses are no longer in use by Anthropic. If you have previously allowlisted these addresses, remove them from your firewall rules.</p>

34.162.102.82/32
34.162.136.91/32
34.162.142.92/32
34.162.183.95/32</code></pre></div><div class="absolute top-3 right-3 print:hidden opacity-100 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover:opacity-100 md:focus-within:opacity-100"><button type="button"
